We can improve by using minimax MW directly:
- Maintain p and u.
- q = conv(p,p) (row payoff for p)
- c = Bp (column loss for u)

- p <- exp(-η_c c)/sum

Now, can this KKT Sinkhorn algorithm actually lower below π/2? Let's reason. It seeks to maximize S with q≤1. The square-root initial has constraints k<n active =1, k≥n slack. The update with u on active k<n: c_i = sum_{j: i+j<n} a_j. For a decreasing square-root, c_i largest for small i? Let's compute i=0: sum_{j=0}^{n-2} a_j ≈S. i=1: sum_{j=0}^{n-3} a_j slightly less. So c decreases with i. Update a_i ∝ 1/c_i, so early coefficients decrease, later increase. This should raise tail, reducing flat first half constraints? q for k<n includes pairs; if early coefficients decrease, q_k maybe decrease for low k; tail increases q_high. Scaling all to max1 may increase S. Likely improves.

What about symmetry? The square-root is one-sided; optimal may be one-sided-ish? The KKT with active constraints may produce some sequence. We should not force symmetry. The problem not symmetric due n? It is symmetric under reversal; one-sided and its reverse both optimal. The algorithm starting one-sided may converge to one-sided optimum. Fine.

Could there be an even better two-sided sequence? Maybe. We can test by also starting from reversed (same) or random perturbations. We can run multiple initializations: square-root, parent, reverse parent, maybe banded modifications. Since the objective is nonconvex, multiple starts help. But time limited. We can run KKT Sinkhorn on a few starts and keep best.

Let's make algorithm robust:

Function `kkt_scale_sequence(a, n, iters, tau, threshold, ...)` returns best p.
Input a nonnegative finite length n. Steps:
1. Clip a≥0; if sum=0, use square-root.
2. Scale a so max_q=1: q=conv(a,a); m=max(q); if m>0: a /= sqrt(m). Else ...
3. S=sum(a); score=2n/S^2 (since m=1) but actual m maybe 1 due scale. Track.
4. For iter in range(max):
   - q=conv(a,a); m=max(q); scale a /= sqrt(m) to make m=1 (if m not 1). After scaling, recompute? Could avoid recompute? The q scaled by 1/m; we can set q = q / m? Since q coefficients scale by 1/m. But for update, need u based on scaled q≤1. We can divide q by m.
   - Determine u weights. Option soft: active = q >= 1 - act_tol. If none (numerical), take top fraction. We can use u = np.zeros_like(q); u[active]=1. Or soft weights: w = np.exp((q - (1-soft_tol))/tau) for q >= 1-soft_tol else 0. Normalize not needed (only ratios matter). If all zero, uniform.
   - Compute c = conv(a[::-1], u, 'full')[n-1:2n-1? wait length. Let's verify indices for c_i = ∑_j u_{i+j} a_j, i=0..n-1. Using v=a[::-1] length n. conv(v,u) length n + (2n-1) -1 =3n-2. c_i = conv_i? Derive earlier: c_i = (v*u)_{i+n-1}. For i=0: conv index n-1 =∑_{a=0}^{n-1} v_a u_{n-1-a} =∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j u_{j}? Let i=0 then u_{0+j}. Since n-1-a =? v_a=a_{n-1-a}. Let j=n-1-a => a_{j}; index n-1-a = n-1-(n-1-j)=j. Sum a_j u_j. But c_0 should ∑_{j:0+j≤2n-2} a_j u_j = all u_j a_j. Good.
For i=1: index n: conv =∑ v_a u_{n-a} =∑ a_j u_{n - a}; j=n-1-a, n-a = j+1. Sum a_j u_{j+1}; c_1 = ∑ a_j u_{j+1}. Good. So c = conv[n-1:n+n-1] length n.
   - Ensure c>0. c_min = max(epsilon, np.min(c)). Use geometric mean gm = np.exp(np.mean(np.log(c))) maybe. Update ratio = gm / c. We can damp with λ: a *= ratio**λ. λ maybe 1.0 or adaptive. If c spans wide, full update may oscillate. Use λ=0.5? We can schedule.
   - After update, clip to [0,1000]? Since unnormalized a maybe coefficients can be large. Score invariant; but final p normalized. Internal a maybe values manageable? Multiplying by constant doesn't affect ratios or q max relative? It scales q and M; we rescale to M=1, so harmless. To keep numbers O(1), after update do `a /= np.median(a)` (if >0) before next q. Then q max not 1 but we scale next. Good.
   - Compute score after scaling to M=1: S=sum(a)*? If we median normalized, need scale to M=1. We can do: after a update and median normalize, compute q=conv(a,a), m=max(q), a_scaled = a / sqrt(m), S=sum(a_scaled), score=2n/S^2. But computing another conv costly. Pseudo:
for it:
  q = conv(a,a)
  m = q.max()
  if m>0: a = a / sqrt(m); q = q / m  # now M=1, q_scaled
  else...
  S = a.sum()
  score = 2*n/(S*S)
  update best if finite.
  if time up: break
  # u based on q (<=1 near)
  u = make_weights(q)
  c = conv(a[::-1], u)[n-1:2n-1]
  c = np.clip(c, tiny, None)
  gm = exp(mean(log(c)))
  ratio = gm / c
  lam = damp
  a *= ratio**lam
  a = np.where(a>0,a, tiny) maybe
  # renormalize median to avoid extreme
  med = np.median(a); if finite >0: a = a/med

def convolve_full(x,y):
    try:
       return sp.signal.fftconvolve(x,y, mode='full')
    except Exception:

```

Now, KKT details more rigorous:
We maximize S = sum a subject a≥0, q_k(a) ≤ 1. Since q homogeneous degree2. However if q max <1, scaling up increases S; we always scale to max=1. The KKT for variables with all positive and active set K:
max S s.t. q_k≤1 for k∈K, q_k<1 otherwise.
Derivative: 1 = ∑_{k∈K} u_k 2 (A_k a)_i. Let B_B with weights v_k (unnormalized) give c_i =∑ v_k (A_k a)_i. Then c_i constant on support (if all positive). Thus u differences. The Sinkhorn update sets c_i constant for given v. The active set determined by q. This is a fixed point. It may correspond to a local max S.

We should maybe use v_k weights not normalized; if v all active equal, c may be large. Update c constant. Then update v based on q. Repeating could converge.

Algorithm `sinkhorn_kkt(a, ...)`:
```
a = np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
n=a.size
a[a<=0]=tiny
# scale to max=1
q=convolve_full(a,a); m=q.max()
if m>0: a/=np.sqrt(m); q/=m
best_p, best_score = norm_score(a)
for it:
  if time: break
  q=convolve_full(a,a); m=q.max()
  if not finite: a=best; break
  if m>0: a = a/np.sqrt(m); q = q/m
  # maybe if max q >1 due round, after scale q<=1.
  S=a.sum(); score=2*n/(S*S)
  if score < best: best = a/S (p) ; best_score=score
  # weights
  # Use tau schedule
  tau = tau_start * tau_end^(it/iters?)
  # active floor: u = exp((q - (1 - margin))/tau) for q >= 1 - margin else 0
  # But if q close 1 for many, okay.
  margin = max(1e-6, 0.1* (q.max()-q.min())?)
```
Let's choose weight formula robust:
We want emphasize q near max (1 after scaling). Let maxq = q.max() (should 1). Let minq = q.min() maybe 1e-6.

```
But after candidate evaluation, we can re-scale.
- Have `best_a` maybe normalized p? We'll store p = a_sum1. To evaluate score p: `q=conv(p,p); maxb=q.max(); score=2*n*maxb` (since sum=1). Or using a: score=2*n/max? Let's standardize: For p sum1, score=2*n*max(q_p). We can internal compute with p.
When scaling a to max1, p=a/sum(a), max_qp =1/S^2, score=2n/S^2. Good.
- Keep `best_p = a/sum(a)`, `best_score=2n/(a.sum()**2)` after max1.
Loop:
  If time > deadline break.
  q = conv(a,a); m=max(q); if m finite >0: a/=sqrt(m); q/=m.
  S=a.sum(); score=2n/(S*S); update best_p (normalize).
  u = weights(q, power, floor)
  c = conv(a[::-1], u)[n-1:2n-1]
  c = np.where(c>tiny, c, tiny)
  gm = exp(mean(log(c)))
  log_ratio = log(gm) - log(c) = -log(c/gm)
  clip log_ratio to [-4,4]
  a_cand = a * exp(step*log_ratio)
  a_cand = np.where(a_cand>tiny, a_cand, tiny)
  # evaluate candidate score
  q_c = conv(a_cand,a_cand); m_c=q_c.max(); if m_c>0: S_c=a_cand.sum()/sqrt(m_c); score_c=2n/(S_c*S_c)
  if score_c < best_score (or current with tol): accept a_cand; if improvement >? step=min(step*1.15,2); else step*=0.7
  else: step*=0.6; if step<0.05: maybe skip? use smaller.
  # median normalize a to avoid scale drift: med=np.median(a); a/=med
  # power update: if no improvement for many, increase power/damp? We can schedule based on iteration regardless.
  iters no improve track.
```

Let's implement a general `refine_sequence(a, n, time_limit, mode)` that can run KKT maxS and also direct gradient.

- At start, build pool initial sequences:

  * square-root at n_parent (or chosen n) if parent length reasonable.
  * maybe square-root length smaller (1024,2048) if time.

- Run `kkt_refine` on square-root init for n_base for e.g. 70% time.

```
from scipy import signal
def conv_full(x,y):
    x=np.asarray(x); y=np.asarray(y)
    # If small, np.convolve maybe faster
    if x.size * y.size < 200000: return np.convolve(x,y,'full')
    return signal.fftconvolve(x,y,mode='full')
```
For n=4806, x.size*y.size 23M >200k, use FFT. Good.

def internal_score_p(p):
   q=conv_full(p,p); m=float(q.max()); return 2*n*m # since sum p=1
```

def a_to_p(a):
  S=a.sum(); p=a/S; score=2*n/(S*S) if max(a*a)=1
```
If a not max1, compute q to be safe:

def score_a(a):
  q=conv(a,a); m=q.max(); S=a.sum(); return 2*n*m/(S*S)
```
Use for best exact? For final exact use evaluate_sequence on list.

Now KKT function with best maintenance:
```
def kkt_refine(a, n, deadline, p0, power_start=1.0, power_max=50.0, step0=1.0, name=''):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if a.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): a=sqrt_seq(n)
    # scale to max1
    a,q=normalize_max1(a)
    S=a.sum(); score=2*n/(S*S)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_p=a/S; best_score=score; current_score=score
    step=step0; no_imp=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # ensure max1
       q=conv_full(a,a); m=q.max()
       if not finite or m<=0: break
       if m>0:
          a=a/np.sqrt(m); q=q/m
       S=a.sum(); current=2*n/(S*S)
       if current < best_score - 1e-12: update best
       # avoid returning values too small/large? p values <=? okay
       # weights
       power = min(power_max, power_start + it*0.15)
       qnorm = (q - q.min())/(q.max()-q.min()+1e-15)
       u = np.power(qnorm, power)
       u = np.maximum(u, 1e-12)
       # normalize u to mean1? No but to avoid large c? ratio unaffected by scalar; but if u sum huge, c huge, logs maybe; no issue. We can set u /= u.mean() to keep logs around 0.
       umean = u.mean(); if umean>0: u/=umean
       c = conv_full(a[::-1], u)[n-1:2*n-1] # length n
       c = np.maximum(c, 1e-300)
       # if many zero, add uniform
       if c.min() <= 1e-12 * c.mean(): c = c + 1e-12*c.mean()
       logc = np.log(c)
       gm = np.exp(np.mean(logc))
       lr = np.log(gm) - logc
       lr = np.clip(lr, -4, 4)
       a_cand = a * np.exp(step * lr)
       a_cand = np.maximum(a_cand, tiny)
       # evaluate candidate score (scale to max1)
       q_c = conv_full(a_cand,a_cand); m_c = q_c.max(); S_c = a_cand.sum()
       if not finite m_c or m_c<=0 or S_c<=0: reject
       else: score_c=2*n*m_c/(S_c*S_c)
       # acceptance
       if score_c < best_score - 1e-10:

              step=0.5

              ...

```

Square-root coefficient generation:
```
def sqrt_poly(n):
    a=np.empty(n)
    a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
       a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*(i-1)+1)/(2*i)
    # This computes ratio for previous i-1 to i: (2i-1)/(2i)
```
Check i=1: ratio (1)/(2)=0.5 good. For large n values positive.

```
def normalize_pos(arr):
   a=np.asarray(arr,float).reshape(-1)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<=0: return None
   return a/S
```

Direct objective refine (log-gradient) after KKT:
Use p sum1. Since KKT may produce near optimal, a short subgradient can shave. Implementation:
```
def subgrad_refine(p, n, deadline, rng):
   p = p/p.sum(); current=score_p(p); best=p; best_sc=current
   step=0.2
   it=0
   while time<deadline:
      q=conv(p,p); m=q.max(); current=2*n*m; update if current<best
      # weights active top: power from q
      power = min(20,1+0.1*it)
      qnorm=(q-m)/(q-m_min) maybe center around max
      u=...
      c=conv(p[::-1], u)[n-1:n+n]
      # gradient of weighted max? We want reduce coefficients where c high.
      # Use multiplicative p_i *= exp(-eta c_i). eta=1/(p dot c + eps) * step? But p*c = weighted avg q ≈m.
      avg=dot(p,c)
      lr = -step * c / max(avg, eps)
      lr = clip(lr,-2,2)
      p_cand=p*exp(lr); p_cand/=sum
      sc_c=2*n*max(conv(p_cand,p_cand))
      if sc_c<best_sc: accept, step*=1.05 else step*=0.7 stale.
```
But if p is near p with small c values, lr ~ -0.2*(c/avg), ratios O(1), okay.
This gradient uses u weights near active, reducing p_i that participate in active sums. It is similar to KKT but on fixed sum. It might improve.

Block perturbation:
```
def block_perturb(p, rng, width_range):
   cand=p.copy()
   w=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//20)))
   s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w))
   cand[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2, w))
   # optional sym half
   if rng.random()<0.3: cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
   cand=np.maximum(cand,0); S=cand.sum(); if S>0: cand/=S
   return cand
```
Evaluate score; accept if <best. But p may have zeros; okay. This random search can improve local. Run until deadline maybe 30% time.

Convolve helper:
```
def _conv_full(x,y):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64); y=np.asarray(y,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size==0 or y.size==0: return np.array([0.0])
    # For very small use direct
    if x.size*y.size <= 300000:
       return np.convolve(x,y,'full')
    if _have_scipy:
       try:
          return _sig.fftconvolve(x,y,mode='full')
       except Exception:
          pass
    return np.convolve(x,y,'full')
```
Potential `fftconvolve` may return length with tiny imaginary? It returns real. Good.

`_score_p(p, n)` internal:
```
def _score_p(p,n):
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return inf
   p=p/s
   q=_conv_full(p,p)
   m=float(np.max(q))
   if not finite or m<0: m=max(0,m)
   return 2.0*n*m
```
Since p sum normalized. For large n, p values small, q sum 1, max ~1/n.

`_normalize_pos(arr)`:
```
def _make_p(arr,n=None):
   if arr is None: return None
   a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   # trim? If n given not same, use as is.
   if a.size==0: return None
   S=a.sum()
   if not finite or S<=0: return None
   return a/S
```
For square-root:
```
def _sqrt_seq(n):
   a=np.empty(n,float)
   a[0]=1
   for i in range(1,n):
       a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
   S=a.sum()
   return a/S
```
For large n loop 4806 trivial.

Max1 conversion:
```
def _to_max1(p_or_a, n):
   a=np.asarray(p_or_a,float).copy(); a=np.maximum(a,0)
   if a.size !=n or not finite: return None
   S=a.sum()
   if S<=0: return None
   q=_conv_full(a,a); m=float(q.max())
   if not finite or m<=0: return None
   a = a/np.sqrt(m)
   # maybe make p? a max1
   return a
```
But if input p sum1, maxq = score/(2n). Using conv to convert costs. Fine.

it=0; step=0.6; power=1.0; stale=0
while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   # normalize a to max1
   q=_conv_full(a,a); m=float(q.max())
   if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0: break
   a = a / np.sqrt(m); q = q / m
   S=float(a.sum()); cur=2.0*n/(S*S)
   if cur < best_score_local - 1e-12: update best_p_local, best_score_local, reset_a=a.copy()
   # maybe if best_score_local is global, update outer? We'll return.
   if cur > best_score_local + 0.2 and stale > 200: break? maybe reset.
   # weights
   power = min(40.0, 1.0 + 0.15*it)
   qmin=float(q.min()); qmax=float(q.max())
   span = qmax - qmin
   if span <= 1e-15:
       u = np.ones_like(q)
   else:
       qnorm = (q - qmin)/span
       qnorm = np.maximum(qnorm, 1e-12)
       # If power high, use log
       u = np.exp(power * np.log(qnorm))
       u = np.maximum(u, 1e-12)
       # To avoid extreme if power large underflow, add floor
       floor = 1e-10
       u = np.maximum(u, floor)
       mean_u = float(u.mean())
       if mean_u >0: u /= mean_u
   c = _conv_full(a[::-1], u)[n-1:n+n]  # ensure length n
   c = np.asarray(c,float64)
   # clip tiny
   mean_c = float(c.mean())
   if not finite or mean_c <=0: break
   c = np.clip(c, 1e-300, None)
   if c.min() <= 1e-12 * mean_c:
       c = c + 1e-10 * mean_c
   logc = np.log(c)
   ln_mean = float(np.mean(logc))
   lr = ln_mean - logc
   lr = np.clip(lr, -3.0, 3.0)
   a_cand = a * np.exp(step * lr)
   a_cand = np.maximum(a_cand, 1e-300)
   # candidate score
   qc = _conv_full(a_cand, a_cand); mc=float(qc.max()); Sc=float(a_cand.sum())
   if not finite mc or mc<=0 or Sc<=0:
       reject
   else:
       sc_c = 2.0*n*mc/(Sc*Sc)
   accept = False
   if finite sc_c:
      if sc_c < best_score_local - 1e-12:
          accept=True
          a_best_c = a_cand / np.sqrt(mc)
          best_score_local=sc_c; best_p_local=a_best_c / a_best_c.sum(); reset_a=a_best_c.copy()
      elif sc_c < cur - 1e-12:
          accept=True
      elif it < 80 and sc_c < best_score_local + 0.03:
          accept=True
   if accept:
      # set a to candidate ratios, normalized median to avoid drift
      a = a_cand
      med=float(np.median(a)); if med>0: a/=med
      step = min(1.0, step*1.05)
      stale=0
   else:
      step *= 0.7
      stale += 1
      if stale > 60:
          # perturb reset
          noise = rng.normal(0, 0.08, n)
          a = reset_a * np.exp(noise)
          # normalize max1
          mq=_max_conv(a)
          if mq>0: a=a/np.sqrt(mq)
          step=0.5
          power=max(1.0, power*0.7)  # power not used as variable? We'll adjust by reducing it? Since power computed from it; we need a persistent power offset.
          stale=0
      if step < 0.03:
          step=0.5
          # maybe reduce power base? We'll have a variable power_mult.
   it +=1
```

Subgrad function:

def _subgrad_refine(p,n,deadline,best_p,best_score,rng):
    p = np.asarray(p,float).copy(); S=p.sum(); p/=S
    cur=_score_p(p,n); best_p_local=p; best_score_local=cur; reset_p=p.copy()
    if best_p is not None and best_score < cur: use best...
    step=0.3; power=1.0; stale=0; it=0
    while time<deadline:
       q=conv(p,p); m=q.max(); cur=2n*m; update best
       power=min(30, power*1.03+0.01?) or based it
       qmax=m; qmin=min(q)
       qnorm=(q-qmin)/(qmax-qmin+eps); qnorm=np.maximum(qnorm,1e-12)
       u=np.exp(power*np.log(qnorm)); u=np.maximum(u,1e-10); u/=u.mean()
       c=conv(p[::-1],u)[n-1:n+n]
       mean_c=c.mean(); if mean_c<=0 break; c=np.maximum(c,1e-300)
       avg=float(np.dot(p,c))
       if avg<=0: avg=mean_c
       lr = -step * c / avg
       lr=np.clip(lr,-1.5,1.5)
       p_cand=p*np.exp(lr); sc=sum; if sc<=0 reject; else p_cand/=sc
       sc_c=2n*max(conv(p_cand,p_cand))
       accept if sc_c<best_score_local -1e-12 or sc_c<cur -1e-12 or it<50 and sc_c<best+0.02
       if accept: p=p_cand; step=min(1.0, step*1.05); stale=0
       else: step*=0.8; stale++; if stale>80: p=reset_p; step=0.3; power*=0.7; stale=0
       if step<0.02: step=0.3; power*=0.7
       it++
    return best
```
This is similar and may not add much. But okay.

def _local_search(p,n,deadline,best_p,best_score,rng):
   p=best_p normalized; best=...
   wmin=max(2,n//300); wmax=max(3,n//15)
   it=0
   while time<deadline:
      cand=p.copy()
      w=int(rng.integers(wmin,wmax+1)); s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w))
      # maybe two blocks
      cand[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.15,w))
      if rng.random()<0.3:
         s2=int(rng.integers(0,n-w)); w2=...; cand[s2:s2+w2]*=...
      if rng.random()<0.2: cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
      cand=np.maximum(cand,0); S=cand.sum(); if S<=0: continue; cand/=S
      sc=_score_p(cand,n)
      if sc<best_score-1e-12: update p=cand,best
      # if no improve for many, revert to best? p remains best; candidate from best.
      it++
```

def _kkt_refine(a, n, deadline, best_p, best_score, rng):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size != n or a.sum() <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_score
    # initial max1
    q = _conv_full(a,a); m=float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0:
        return best_p, best_score
    a = a/np.sqrt(m)
    S=float(a.sum())
    cur=2.0*n/(S*S)
    local_p=a/S
    local_score=cur
    if best_p is None or local_score < best_score:
        best_p=local_p; best_score=local_score
    # reset_a: best_p max1
    if best_p is None:
        reset_a=a.copy()
    else:
        reset_a=_p_to_max1(best_p,n)
        if reset_a is None: reset_a=a.copy()
    step=0.6; power=1.0; stale=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       ...
       # normalize work a to max1 and compute cur
       q = _conv_full(a,a); m=float(q.max())
       if not finite or m<=0: break
       a = a/np.sqrt(m); q = q/m
       S=float(a.sum()); cur=2.0*n/(S*S)
       if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=cur; best_p=a/S; reset_a=a.copy()
       # if current much worse than best and stale high, maybe reset before update? not needed
       # weights
       qmax=float(q.max()); qmin=float(q.min())
       span=qmax-qmin
       if span <= 1e-15:
           u=np.ones_like(q)
       else:
           qnorm=(q-qmin)/span
           qnorm=np.maximum(qnorm, 1e-12)
           with np.errstate(...): u=np.exp(power*np.log(qnorm))
           u=np.maximum(u, 1e-12)
           u=np.maximum(u, 1e-10) # floor absolute
           mu=float(u.mean())
           if mu>0: u/=mu
       c=_conv_full(a[::-1], u)
       if c.size < n+n-1: ... maybe direct
       c=c[n-1:n+n]
       if c.size != n: c=np.resize(c,n) # safety
       c=np.asarray(c,float); c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0); c=np.maximum(c, _TINY)
       mc=float(c.mean())
       if not finite or mc<=0: break
       if c.min() <= 1e-12*mc:
           c = c + 1e-10*mc
       logc=np.log(np.maximum(c, _TINY))
       ln_mean=float(np.mean(logc))
       lr=ln_mean - logc
       lr=np.clip(lr, -3.0, 3.0)
       a_cand=a*np.exp(step*lr)
       a_cand=np.where(np.isfinite(a_cand), a_cand, 0.0)
       a_cand=np.maximum(a_cand, _TINY)
       Sc=float(a_cand.sum())
       if Sc <=0: reject...
       qc=_conv_full(a_cand,a_cand); mc_q=float(qc.max())
       if not finite mc_q or mc_q <=0: reject
       sc_c=2.0*n*mc_q/(Sc*Sc)
       accept=False
       if np.isfinite(sc_c):
           if sc_c < best_score - 1e-12:
               accept=True
               a_best=a_cand/np.sqrt(mc_q)
               Sb=float(a_best.sum())
               if Sb>0:
                  best_score=sc_c; best_p=a_best/Sb; reset_a=a_best.copy()
           elif sc_c < cur - 1e-12:
               accept=True
           elif it < 100 and sc_c < best_score + 0.05:
               accept=True
       if accept:
           a=a_cand
           med=float(np.median(a))
           if med>0 and np.isfinite(med): a=a/med
           if sc_c <= best_score + 1e-12:
               step=min(1.0, step*1.05)
           else:
               step=min(1.0, step*1.02)
           stale=0
           # increase power slowly
           power=min(60.0, power*1.03 + 0.01)
       else:
           step *= 0.7
           stale += 1
           if stale > 80:
               # perturb reset
               if reset_a is not None and reset_a.size==n:
                   noise=rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=n)
                   a=reset_a*np.exp(noise)
                   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
                   qtmp=_conv_full(a,a); mt=float(qtmp.max())
                   if np.isfinite(mt) and mt>0:
                       q = qtmp/mt
                       a = a/np.sqrt(mt)
                       S=float(a.sum()); cur=2.0*n/(S*S)

                           best_score=cur; best_p=a/S; reset_a=a.copy()
                   step=0.5

                   stale=0

               else:
                   break
           if step < 0.03:
               step=0.5
               if stale > 40:
                   power=max(1.0, power*0.8)
       it += 1
    return best_p,best_score

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy import signal as _scipy_signal
except Exception:
    _scipy_signal = None

_TINY = 1e-300
_EPS = 1e-15


def _conv_full(x, y):
    """Full 1-D convolution, using FFT when large and direct convolution when small."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if x.size == 0 or y.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not (np.all(np.isfinite(x)) and np.all(np.isfinite(y))):
        return np.zeros(max(x.size + y.size - 1, 1), dtype=np.float64)

    # Direct numpy convolution is competitive for very small cases.
    if x.size * y.size <= 300_000:
        return np.convolve(x, y, mode="full").astype(np.float64, copy=False)

    if _scipy_signal is not None:
        try:
            return np.asarray(_scipy_signal.fftconvolve(x, y, mode="full"), dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        return np.convolve(x, y, mode="full").astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    except Exception:
        return np.zeros(x.size + y.size - 1, dtype=np.float64)


def _make_p(arr):
    """Convert an arbitrary sequence to a finite nonnegative NumPy array with sum 1."""
    if arr is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a / s


def _sqrt_sequence_p(n):
    """
    Nonnegative sequence from the coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), normalized to sum 1.
    For large n its AC1 score tends to pi/2, already below 2.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (2 * i - 1) / (2 * i)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a / s


def _score_p_internal(p, n=None):
    """Internal score for p already (or after) normalized to sum 1."""
    if p is None:
        return math.inf
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    if n is None:
        n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return math.inf
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        a = a / s
    q = _conv_full(a, a)
    if q.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _p_to_max_convolution(p, n):
    """Return a scaled version of p whose full self-convolution max coefficient is 1."""
    if p is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size != int(n):
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    a = a / s
    q = _conv_full(a, a)
    if q.size == 0:
        return None
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a / math.sqrt(m)


def _kkt_refine(a_initial, n, deadline, best_p, best_score, rng):
    """
    Sinkhorn/KKT-style refinement.

    Optimizes the equivalent scale-invariant problem:
        maximize sum(a) subject to a >= 0 and max_k (a*a)_k <= 1.
    The score is 2*n / (sum(a))^2 when the convolution maximum is 1.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.asarray(a_initial, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if a.size != n:
        return best_p, best_score
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s0 = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s0) or s0 <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_score

    q = _conv_full(a, a)
    m = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        a = _sqrt_sequence_p(n)
        if a is None:
            return best_p, best_score
        q = _conv_full(a, a)
        m = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            return best_p, best_score

    a = a / math.sqrt(m)
    S = float(a.sum())
    cur = float(2.0 * n / (S * S))
    p_cur = a / S

    if best_p is None or cur < best_score:
        best_p = p_cur
        best_score = cur
        reset_a = a.copy()
    else:
        reset_a = _p_to_max_convolution(best_p, n)
        if reset_a is None:
            reset_a = a.copy()

    step = 0.60
    power = 1.0
    stale = 0
    it = 0

    while True:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        # Normalize work point so max convolution coefficient is 1.
        q = _conv_full(a, a)
        if q.size == 0:
            break
        m = float(np.max(q))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            a = reset_a.copy()
            q = _conv_full(a, a)
            m = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
                break
            a = a / math.sqrt(m)
            q = q / m
        else:
            a = a / math.sqrt(m)
            q = q / m

        S = float(a.sum())
        cur = float(2.0 * n / (S * S)) if S > 0 else math.inf
        if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur
            best_p = a / S
            reset_a = a.copy()

        # Dual weights emphasizing coefficients near the active maximum.
        qmin = float(np.min(q))
        qmax = float(np.max(q))
        span = qmax - qmin
        if not np.isfinite(span) or span <= _EPS:
            u = np.ones_like(q, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            qnorm = (q - qmin) / span
            qnorm = np.maximum(qnorm, 1e-12)
            with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
                u = np.exp(power * np.log(qnorm))
            u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
            u = np.maximum(u, 1e-12)
            u = np.maximum(u, 1e-10)
            mu = float(u.mean())
            if not np.isfinite(mu) or mu <= 0.0:
                u = np.ones_like(q, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                u = u / mu

        # c_i = sum_j u_{i+j} a_j, a weighted stationarity residual.
        c = _conv_full(a[::-1], u)
        if c.size < n:
            break
        c = c[n - 1:n + n]
        if c.size != n:
            tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            tmp[:c.size] = c[:c.size]
            c = tmp
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        c = np.maximum(c, _TINY)
        mean_c = float(c.mean())
        if not np.isfinite(mean_c) or mean_c <= 0.0:
            break
        if c.min() <= 1e-12 * mean_c:
            c = c + 1e-10 * mean_c

        with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            logc = np.log(np.maximum(c, _TINY))
        logc = np.where(np.isfinite(logc), logc, 0.0)
        ln_mean = float(np.mean(logc))
        if not np.isfinite(ln_mean):
            break

        lr = ln_mean - logc
        lr = np.clip(lr, -3.0, 3.0)

        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            a_cand = a * np.exp(step * lr)
        a_cand = np.where(np.isfinite(a_cand), a_cand, 0.0)
        a_cand = np.maximum(a_cand, _TINY)
        Sc = float(a_cand.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(Sc) or Sc <= 0.0:
            a_cand = a.copy()
            Sc = float(a_cand.sum())

        qc = _conv_full(a_cand, a_cand)
        mc = float(np.max(qc)) if qc.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc <= 0.0:
            accept = False
            sc_c = math.inf
            a_best_cand = None
        else:
            sc_c = float(2.0 * n * mc / (Sc * Sc))
            a_best_cand = a_cand / math.sqrt(mc) if sc_c < best_score else None
            accept = False

        if np.isfinite(sc_c):
            if sc_c < best_score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
                Sb = float(a_best_cand.sum()) if a_best_cand is not None else 0.0
                if Sb > 0.0 and np.isfinite(Sb):
                    best_score = sc_c
                    best_p = a_best_cand / Sb
                    reset_a = a_best_cand.copy()
            elif sc_c < cur - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif it < 120 and sc_c < best_score + 0.05:
                # Early controlled exploration.
                accept = True

        if accept:
            a = a_cand
            med = float(np.median(a))
            if np.isfinite(med) and med > 0.0:
                a = a / med
            if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c <= cur + 1e-12:
                step = min(1.0, step * 1.05)
            else:
                step = min(1.0, step * 1.02)
            stale = 0
            power = min(60.0, power * 1.03 + 0.01)
        else:
            step *= 0.70
            stale += 1
            if stale > 80:
                if reset_a is not None and reset_a.size == n:
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=n)
                    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
                        a = reset_a * np.exp(noise)
                    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
                    a = np.maximum(a, _TINY)
                    qt = _conv_full(a, a)
                    mt = float(np.max(qt)) if qt.size else 0.0
                    if np.isfinite(mt) and mt > 0.0:
                        a = a / math.sqrt(mt)
                        St = float(a.sum())
                        ct = float(2.0 * n / (St * St)) if St > 0 else math.inf
                        if np.isfinite(ct) and ct < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_score = ct
                            best_p = a / St
                            reset_a = a.copy()
                    step = 0.5
                    power = max(1.0, power * 0.70)
                    stale = 0
                    it += 25
                else:
                    break
            if step < 0.03:
                step = 0.5
                if stale > 40:
                    power = max(1.0, power * 0.80)

        it += 1

    return best_p, best_score


def _subgradient_refine(p, n, deadline, best_p, best_score, rng):
    """
    Short projected multiplicative-gradient polish directly on sum-normalized p.
    """
    if p is None:
        p = best_p
    if p is None:
        return best_p, best_score

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if p.size != int(n):
        return best_p, best_score
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_score
    p = p / s

    cur = _score_p_internal(p, n)
    if cur < best_score:
        best_score = cur
        best_p = p.copy()

    reset_p = best_p.copy()
    step = 0.35
    power = 1.0
    stale = 0
    it = 0
    cur_p = p.copy()
    cur_score = cur

    while True:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        n_local = cur_p.size
        q = _conv_full(cur_p, cur_p)
        if q.size == 0:
            break
        m = float(np.max(q))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            cur_p = reset_p.copy()
            cur_score = best_score
            continue
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n_local * m)
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = cur_p.copy()
            reset_p = cur_p.copy()

        qmin = float(np.min(q))
        span = m - qmin
        if not np.isfinite(span) or span <= _EPS:
            u = np.ones_like(q, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            qnorm = (q - qmin) / span
            qnorm = np.maximum(qnorm, 1e-12)
            with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
                u = np.exp(power * np.log(qnorm))
            u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
            u = np.maximum(u, 1e-10)
            mu = float(u.mean())
            if not np.isfinite(mu) or mu <= 0.0:
                u = np.ones_like(q, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                u = u / mu

        c = _conv_full(cur_p[::-1], u)
        if c.size < n_local:
            break
        c = c[n_local - 1:2 * n_local - 1]
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        c = np.maximum(c, _TINY)
        avg = float(np.dot(cur_p, c))
        if not np.isfinite(avg) or avg <= 0.0:
            avg = float(c.mean())
        if not np.isfinite(avg) or avg <= 0.0:
            break

        lr = -step * (c / avg)
        lr = np.clip(lr, -1.75, 1.75)
        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            p_cand = cur_p * np.exp(lr)
        p_cand = np.where(np.isfinite(p_cand), p_cand, 0.0)
        p_cand = np.maximum(p_cand, _TINY)
        sc = float(p_cand.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc <= 0.0:
            p_cand = cur_p.copy()
            sc = 1.0
        p_cand = p_cand / sc

        sc_cand = _score_p_internal(p_cand, n_local)
        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(sc_cand):
            if sc_cand < best_score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif sc_cand < cur_score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif it < 60 and sc_cand < best_score + 0.03:
                accept = True

        if accept:
            cur_p = p_cand
            cur_score = sc_cand
            step = min(1.0, step * 1.04)
            power = min(40.0, power * 1.02 + 0.005)
            stale = 0
        else:
            step *= 0.75
            stale += 1
            if stale > 60:
                cur_p = reset_p.copy()
                cur_score = best_score
                step = 0.35
                power = max(1.0, power * 0.75)
                stale = 0
            if step < 0.02:
                step = 0.35
                power = max(1.0, power * 0.85)

        it += 1

    return best_p, best_score


def _local_block_search(p, n, deadline, best_p, best_score, rng):
    """Hill-climbing block multiplicative refinement around the current best."""
    if p is None:
        p = best_p
    if p is None:
        return best_p, best_score

    n = int(max(1, n))
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if p.size != n:
        return best_p, best_score
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_score
    p = p / s

    cur = _score_p_internal(p, n)
    if cur < best_score:
        best_score = cur
        best_p = p.copy()

    cur_p = best_p.copy()
    wmin = max(2, n // 700)
    wmax = max(wmin + 1, n // 25)
    stale = 0

    while True:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        cand = cur_p.copy()
        blocks = int(rng.integers(1, 3))
        for _ in range(blocks):
            w = int(rng.integers(wmin, wmax + 1))
            if w >= n:
                w = max(1, n // 2)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w)) if n > w else 0
            stop = min(n, start + w)
            factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.14)))
            cand[start:stop] *= max(1e-12, factor)

        if rng.random() < 0.22:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])

        cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        cs = float(cand.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(cs) or cs <= 0.0:
            continue
        cand = cand / cs

        sc = _score_p_internal(cand, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = cand.copy()
            cur_p = cand.copy()
            stale = 0
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale > 120:
                cur_p = best_p.copy()
                stale = 0

        if stale > 500:
            break

    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    """
    Returns a non-empty list of finite nonnegative coefficients minimizing the
    authoritative evaluator's AC1 upper bound under the available time budget.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(0.0, budget_s))
    # Leave headroom for final exact evaluation and Python return.
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 30.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_p = None
    best_score = math.inf

    # Optional parent construction.  Referenced directly, never copied as a literal.
    parent_p = None
    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        parent_raw = None
    if parent_raw is not None:
        parent_p = _make_p(parent_raw)
        if parent_p is not None:
            sc = _score_p_internal(parent_p)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = parent_p.copy()

    # Choose a working length.  If parent exists and is reasonable, use its length.
    target_n = 4806
    if best_p is not None:
        ln = int(best_p.size)
        if 64 <= ln <= 20000:
            target_n = ln

    # Strong analytic fallback: truncation of (1-z)^(-1/2).
    sqrt_p = _sqrt_sequence_p(target_n)
    if sqrt_p is not None:
        sc = _score_p_internal(sqrt_p, target_n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = sqrt_p.copy()

    # If no valid candidate was made, guarantee one.
    if best_p is None or best_p.size == 0:
        best_p = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = _score_p_internal(best_p, best_p.size)

    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()

    # Primary large refinement: start from the best known length-target point.
    primary_p = None
    if best_p is not None and best_p.size == target_n:
        primary_p = best_p
    elif sqrt_p is not None and sqrt_p.size == target_n:
        primary_p = sqrt_p

    if primary_p is not None and remaining > 70.0:
        a_init = _p_to_max_convolution(primary_p, target_n)
        if a_init is not None:
            deadline_kkt = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, 0.62 * remaining)
            best_p, best_score = _kkt_refine(
                a_init, target_n, deadline_kkt, best_p, best_score, rng
            )

    # Secondary refinement: if parent was not the primary, try it as a different start.
    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    secondary_p = None
    if parent_p is not None and parent_p.size == target_n:
        # Avoid obviously identical object if primary was parent.
        if not (primary_p is not None and
                primary_p.size == parent_p.size and
                np.array_equal(primary_p, parent_p, equal_nan=False)):
            secondary_p = parent_p
    elif sqrt_p is not None and sqrt_p.size == target_n:
        if not (primary_p is not None and
                primary_p.size == sqrt_p.size and
                np.array_equal(primary_p, sqrt_p, equal_nan=False)):
            secondary_p = sqrt_p

    if secondary_p is not None and remaining > 120.0:
        a_init = _p_to_max_convolution(secondary_p, target_n)
        if a_init is not None:
            deadline_kkt2 = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, 0.18 * remaining)
            best_p, best_score = _kkt_refine(
                a_init, target_n, deadline_kkt2, best_p, best_score, rng
            )

    # Moderate gradient polish on the best target-length point.
    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 90.0 and best_p is not None and best_p.size == target_n:
        deadline_sub = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, 0.13 * remaining)
        best_p, best_score = _subgradient_refine(
            best_p, target_n, deadline_sub, best_p, best_score, rng
        )

    # Final short local search, using whatever time is left but stopping before deadline.
    deadline_local = hard_deadline - 5.0
    if best_p is not None and best_p.size == target_n and deadline_local > time.monotonic():
        best_p, best_score = _local_block_search(
            best_p, target_n, deadline_local, best_p, best_score, rng
        )

    # Build a small candidate pool and choose by the authoritative exact evaluator.
    pool = []

    def _add_candidate(c):
        try:
            if c is None:
                return
            arr = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if arr.size == 0:
                return
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                return
            pool.append(arr)
        except Exception:
            return

    _add_candidate(parent_p)
    _add_candidate(sqrt_p)
    _add_candidate(best_p)
    if not pool:
        pool.append(np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64))

    # Avoid redundant exact evaluations for identical arrays when possible.
    def _signature(arr):
        try:
            return (int(arr.size), tuple(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)[:8]))
        except Exception:
            return (int(-1), id(arr))

    seen = set()
    best_list = None
    best_exact = math.inf

    for arr in pool:
        try:
            lst = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)]
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not lst:
            continue
        try:
            sig = _signature(lst)
        except Exception:
            sig = None
        if sig is not None:
            if sig in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(sig)
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            sc = math.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = sc
            best_list = lst

    if best_list is None or not best_list:
        best_list = [1.0]

    return best_list
```
